1: const $ = (s) => typeof s === "string" && s.trim() ? document.querySelector(s) : null,
2:   $$ = (s) => typeof s === "string" && s.trim() ? [...document.querySelectorAll(s)] : [];
3: const today = new Date(),
4:   dateKey = (d) =>
5:     `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
6: const formatSyncTime = (value) => {
7:   if (!value) return "조회 전";
8:   const parsed = new Date(value);
9:   if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return String(value);
10:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ko-KR", { month:"2-digit", day:"2-digit", hour:"2-digit", minute:"2-digit", hour12:false }).format(parsed);
11: };
12: const addDateDays = (dateText, days) => {
13:   const date = new Date(`${dateText || dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
14:   if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "";
15:   date.setDate(date.getDate() + Number(days || 0));
16:   return dateKey(date);
17: };
18: const daysFromToday = (dateText) => {
19:   const due = new Date(`${dateText}T12:00:00`), now = new Date(`${dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
20:   return Number.isNaN(due.getTime()) ? null : Math.round((due-now)/86400000);
21: };

347: async function withStartupTimeout(promise, milliseconds, fallback = null) {
348:   let timer;
349:   try {
350:     return await Promise.race([
351:       Promise.resolve(promise),
352:       new Promise((resolve) => { timer = setTimeout(() => resolve(fallback), milliseconds); }),
353:     ]);
354:   } finally {
355:     if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
356:   }
357: }

488: async function migrateAdminProgramOverridesToSheetOnce(){

794: async function sensitiveHash(value){
795:   const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode(`UEP:SENSITIVE:${String(value||'')}`);
796:   const digest=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',bytes);
797:   return Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest)).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
798: }

1146: function calendarEventIsPending(event) {
1147:   return /예약|예정|미정|미기재|검토|대기/.test(String(event?.status || ""));

1150: function calendarEventTargetsFirstGrade(event) {
1151:   const target = String(event?.target || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
1152:   const detail = String(event?.detail || "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").replace(/\(?1\s*[,·ㆍ&/]?\s*2?\s*학년부[^)]*\)?/g, " ");
1153:   if (/학부모/.test(target) && !/학생/.test(target)) return false;
1154:   if (/교사|교직원/.test(target) && !/학생/.test(target)) return false;
1155:   const text = target || detail;
1156:   return /(?:^|[^0-9])1\s*학년/.test(text)
1157:     || /1\s*[,·ㆍ&/]\s*2\s*학년/.test(text)
1158:     || /1\s*[~-]\s*3\s*학년/.test(text)
1159:     || /전교생|전체\s*학생/.test(text);
1160: }

1163:   const text = `${event?.title || ""} ${event?.target || ""} ${event?.detail || ""}`;
1164:   if (/(정기고사|기말고사|중간고사|방학식|개학식|교직원회의|부장교사회의|운영위원회|석식\s*[Xx]|급식제공|시간표\s*운영|교실개방|생활기록부.*점검|입찰|폐지\(|폐기물|제안서.*제출기간)/.test(text)) return false;
1165:   return /(프로그램|특강|체험|교육|설명회|공연|상담|탐방|캠프|연수|활동|동아리|수련|수학여행|대회|촬영|학습코칭|석학과의 만남)/.test(text);
1166: }

1174: function calendarEventMatchesFilter(event) {

1180: function calendarDisplayTitle(event) {
1181:   const title=String(event?.title||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
1182:   const raw=String(event?.originalDetail||event?.detail||'').replace(/\r/g,'');
1183:   const parts=raw.split(/[\n/]+/).map(x=>x.replace(/^\([^)]*\)\s*/,'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
1184:   const looksGeneric = !title || /^(?:\(?\d+[-~]?\d*교시\)?|\(?\d+[-~]\d+교시\)?|~|\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?:[-~]\d{1,2}:\d{2})?|\d+학년|각 반|전체)$/.test(title);
1185:   const candidate=parts.find(x=>!/(?:^\d{1,2}:\d{2}|^\d+학년$|교시$|희망학생|전체$|각 반|교실$|세미나실$|고당예관$|확정$|예정$|예약$|부$)/.test(x));
1186:   if (looksGeneric && candidate) return candidate;
1187:   return title || candidate || '일정';
1188: }

1192:   return value.length > max ? `${value.slice(0, max)}…` : value;
1193: }

1195: function calendarDayTone(key) {

1197:   const weekday = date.getDay();
1198:   const holiday = attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(key);
1199:   return {
1200:     holiday,
1201:     saturday: weekday === 6 && !holiday,
1202:     sunday: weekday === 0 && !holiday,
1203:     className: holiday ? "holiday" : weekday === 6 ? "saturday" : weekday === 0 ? "sunday" : "weekday",
1204:   };
1205: }

1421: const STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_STORE='uep-student-card-heat-v2';
1422: const STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_PERIOD_STORE='uep-student-card-heat-period-v1';
1423: function studentCardHeatId(student){return String(student?.id||student?.studentNo||'').trim();}
1424: function studentCardHeatPeriodDays(){try{return localStorage.getItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_PERIOD_STORE)==='7'?7:30;}catch{return 30;}}
1425: function setStudentCardHeatPeriodDays(days){const value=Number(days)===7?7:30;try{localStorage.setItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_PERIOD_STORE,String(value));}catch{}return value;}
1426: function readStudentCardHeatStore(){try{const raw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_STORE)||'{}');return raw&&typeof raw==='object'&&!Array.isArray(raw)?raw:{};}catch{return {};}}
1427: function writeStudentCardHeatStore(store){try{localStorage.setItem(STUDENT_CARD_HEAT_STORE,JSON.stringify(store||{}));}catch{}}
1428: function studentCardHeat(student){const id=studentCardHeatId(student);if(!id)return 0;const store=readStudentCardHeatStore(),cutoff=Date.now()-studentCardHeatPeriodDays()*86400000;const recent=(Array.isArray(store[id])?store[id]:[]).map(Number).filter(t=>Number.isFinite(t)&&t>=cutoff);return recent.length;}
1429: function touchStudentCardHeat(student){const id=studentCardHeatId(student);if(!id)return 0;const store=readStudentCardHeatStore(),history=(Array.isArray(store[id])?store[id]:[]).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite);history.push(Date.now());store[id]=history.filter(t=>t>=Date.now()-31*86400000).slice(-80);writeStudentCardHeatStore(store);return studentCardHeat(student);}
1430: function studentHeatClass(student){const n=studentCardHeat(student);return n>=7?'heat-7':n===6?'heat-6':n===5?'heat-5':n===4?'heat-4':n===3?'heat-3':n===2?'heat-2':n===1?'heat-1':'heat-0';}
1431: function studentIsCurrentlyActive(student){
1432:   const status=String(student?.status||student?.enrollmentStatus||'재학').trim();
1433:   return status==='재학'||status==='전입'||(!status);
1434: }

1479: const NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE='uep-night-study-rules-v1';
1480: function defaultNightStudyRules(){
1481:   return [
1482:     {id:'weekly-wed',name:'수요일 미운영',type:'weekday',weekday:3,active:true},
1483:     {id:'summer-2026',name:'2026 여름방학 미운영',type:'range',start:'2026-08-08',end:'2026-08-17',active:true}
1484:   ];

1486: function readNightStudyRules(){
1487:   try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE)||'null');return Array.isArray(saved)?saved:defaultNightStudyRules();}
1488:   catch{return defaultNightStudyRules();}

1490: function nightStudyOperationStatus(key){
1491:   const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
1492:   const rules=readNightStudyRules().filter(r=>r&&r.active!==false);
1493:   for(const r of rules){
1494:     if(r.type==='range'&&r.start&&r.end&&key>=r.start&&key<=r.end)return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'미운영 기간'};
1495:     if(r.type==='weekday'&&Number(r.weekday)===date.getDay())return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'요일 미운영'};
1496:   }
1497:   return {operates:true,reason:'운영일'};
1498: }

1519: function dormRuleHistory(){
1520:   const sheetRules=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormRules)?readonlyCache.dormRules:[];
1521:   if(sheetRules.length) return sheetRules.map(x=>({...x,active:x.active!==false}));
1522:   return dormDefaultRules().map(x=>({...x,active:x.active!==false,source:'UEP 기본값(시트 연결 대기)'}));
1523: }
1524: function setDormRuleCache(rules){
1525:   readonlyCache=readonlyCache||{};
1526:   readonlyCache.dormRules=(Array.isArray(rules)?rules:[]).map(x=>({...x}));
1527: }

1529: // v0.76.97: 학사외출 규칙 읽기는 일반 UEP readonly 동기화 경로 하나만 사용합니다.
1530: function refreshDormRulesFromReadonly({rerender=true,silent=true}={}){
1531:   const rules=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormRules)?readonlyCache.dormRules:[];
1532:   if(rerender&&dormMode==='outing')render('dorm');
1533:   if(!silent)toast(rules.length?`학사운영규칙 ${rules.length}건이 연결되어 있습니다.`:'학사운영규칙을 찾지 못했습니다.');
1534:   return rules.length>0;
1535: }

1537: function dormRuleCategoryLabel(category){
1538:   return ({period:'학교 운영기간',closure:'학사 미운영기간',exception:'입·퇴소 변경',exam:'시험 외출허용',vacation:'방학 중 외출허용',base:'기본 입·퇴소'})[category]||'기타 규칙';
1539: }
1540: function dormRuleNormalizeDateTime(value){
1541:   if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='') return '';
1542:   // Google Sheets 날짜 직렬값은 시트의 '현지 날짜/시간' 값이다.
1543:   // UTC instant로 해석한 뒤 Asia/Seoul을 다시 적용하면 +9시간이 생기므로,
1544:   // 직렬값의 날짜/시간 성분을 그대로 추출한다.
1545:   if(typeof value==='number'||/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(String(value).trim())){
1546:     const serial=Number(value);
1547:     if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
1548:       const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
1549:       if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
1550:         const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),m=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
1551:         const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
1552:         return `${y}-${m}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
1553:       }
1554:     }
1555:   }
1556:   const raw=String(value).trim().replace(' ','T');
1557:   const m=raw.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
1558:   if(m) return `${m[1]}-${String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(m[3]).padStart(2,'0')}T${String(m[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${m[5]}`;
1559:   return String(value).trim();
1560: }
1561: function dormRuleDateText(value){
1562:   const v=dormRuleNormalizeDateTime(value);
1563:   return v?`${v.slice(0,10)}${v.length>10?` ${v.slice(11,16)}`:''}`:'-';
1564: }
1565: function dormRuleAppliesAt(rule,dateLike){
1566:   if(!rule?.active)return false;
1567:   const stamp=(dateLike instanceof Date?dateLike:new Date(dateLike)).getTime();
1568:   const a=new Date(rule.start||'1900-01-01T00:00').getTime(), b=new Date(rule.end||'2999-12-31T23:59').getTime();
1569:   return Number.isFinite(stamp)&&stamp>=a&&stamp<=b;
1570: }
1571: function dormOutingIsMedical(item){
1572:   const text=[item?.category,item?.reason,item?.destination,item?.type,item?.outingType].map(v=>String(v||'').trim()).join(' ');
1573:   return /병원|의원|진료|치과|한의원|의료/.test(text);
1574: }
1575: function dormOutingExcludedByRule(item){
1576:   // 병원 외출은 학사운영규칙의 학원외출 허용/미운영 판정과 무관한 개인 외출사유입니다.
1577:   // 신청이 존재하면 오늘의 학사외출현황과 메일/출력 대상에서 제외하지 않습니다.
1578:   if(dormOutingIsMedical(item))return false;
1579:   if(item?.operationIncluded===false)return true;
1580:   const value=String(item?.date||'').slice(0,10);
1581:   if(!value)return false;
1582:   const stamp=new Date(`${value}T12:00:00`);
1583:   return dormRuleHistory().some(rule=>rule.category==='closure'&&dormRuleAppliesAt(rule,stamp));
1584: }
1585: function dormRuleOperationStateForCategory(category){
1586:   return ({period:'운영',closure:'미운영',base:'운영',exception:'입퇴소변경',exam:'개인외출허용',vacation:'개인외출허용'})[category]||'';
1587: }
1588: function dormRuleEffectDefault(category){
1589:   return ({period:'운영기간',closure:'학사 미운영(퇴소~입소 자동기간)',base:'기본 주간운영',exception:'입퇴소 변경(외출허용 기본상태 유지)',exam:'학원외출 특별허용',vacation:'방학 중 학원외출 특별허용'})[category]||'';
1590: }
1591: function dormRuleWeekdaysFromRange(start,end){
1592:   const a=new Date(start),b=new Date(end);if(Number.isNaN(a.getTime())||Number.isNaN(b.getTime()))return '전체';
1593:   const names=['일','월','화','수','목','금','토'],seen=[];
1594:   const d=new Date(a);d.setHours(12,0,0,0);const stop=new Date(b);stop.setHours(12,0,0,0);
1595:   let guard=0;while(d<=stop&&guard++<8){const n=names[d.getDay()];if(!seen.includes(n))seen.push(n);d.setDate(d.getDate()+1);}
1596:   return guard>=8?'전체':seen.join(',');
1597: }
1598: function applyDormRuleEditorMode(){
1599:   const category=$('#dormRuleCategory')?.value||'exception';
1600:   const visible={period:['start','end','note','priority'],closure:['actualExit','actualReentry','late','reason','priorHome','form','note','priority'],base:['start','end','days','entry','exit','late','early','reason','priorHome','form','note','priority'],exception:['actualExit','actualReentry','late','reason','priorHome','form','note','priority'],exam:['start','end','days','late','reason','form','individual','note','priority'],vacation:['start','end','days','late','early','reason','priorHome','form','individual','note','priority']}[category]||[];
1601:   $$('[data-dorm-rule-field]').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('hidden',!visible.includes(el.dataset.dormRuleField)));
1602:   const effect=$('#dormRuleEffect');if(effect&&!effect.value)effect.value=dormRuleEffectDefault(category);
1603:   if(['closure','exception'].includes(category)&&$('#dormRuleDays'))$('#dormRuleDays').value=dormRuleWeekdaysFromRange($('#dormRuleActualExit')?.value,$('#dormRuleActualReentry')?.value);
1604: }

1610:     : (existing||{id:`DORM-RULE-${Date.now()}`,category:'exception',name:'',start:'',end:'',days:'전체',effect:'입·퇴소 변경 검토',entryTime:'',exitTime:'',lateEntry:'',earlyExit:'',reason:'',priority:50,note:'',active:true,source:'UEP 규칙센터',operationState:'',actualExit:'',actualReentry:'',priorNightAcademyHome:false,formRequired:false,individualOutingAllowed:false});

1758: function dormTermRoster(mode){
1759:   const term=dormTermDefinition(mode);if(!term)return [];
1760:   const all=[...(readonlyCache?.dormHistory||[]),...(readonlyCache?.pastDormStudents||[]),...(readonlyCache?.dormStudents||[])];
1761:   const seen=new Set();
1762:   return all.filter(row=>{
1763:     const year=String(row.year||'').replace(/\.0$/,'');
1764:     const sem=String(row.semester||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
1765:     const no=String(row.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
1766:     const inferredGrade=no?Number(no[0]):Number(String(row.className||'').match(/^([123])[- ]/)?.[1]||1);
1767:     const grade=Number(row.grade||inferredGrade||1);
1768:     if(year&&term.year&&year!==term.year)return false;
1769:     if(grade!==term.grade)return false;
1770:     if(term.semester&&sem&&sem!==term.semester)return false;
1771:     if(term.semester&&!sem){
1772:       const start=String(row.startDate||'');
1773:       if(start){const month=Number(start.slice(5,7));if(term.semester==='1'&&month>=8)return false;if(term.semester==='2'&&month>0&&month<7)return false;}
1774:     }
1775:     const key=`${row.studentId||row.studentNo}|${term.label}`;if(seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true;
1776:   }).sort(dormStudentNoSort);
1777: }

1882:   return `<section class="dorm-career-panel"><div class="dorm-career-summary"><span><small>진로희망계열</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.hopeTrack)}</b></span><span><small>희망전공·학과</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.hopeMajor)}</b></span><span><small>대학적성</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.aptitude)}</b></span><span><small>UEP 계열 참고</small><b>${escapeHtml(career.recommendation)}</b></span></div><div class="dorm-selection-list">${groups||'<div class="empty-inline">선택과목 자료가 아직 없습니다.</div>'}</div></section>`;
1883: }
1884: function dormProgramTermKey(program){
1885:   const explicit=String(program.semester||program.term||'');
1886:   if(/2학년\s*1|2-1/.test(explicit))return 'g2s1';
1887:   if(/2학년\s*2|2-2/.test(explicit))return 'g2s2';
1888:   if(/3학년/.test(explicit))return 'g3';
1889:   const date=String(program.date||program.startDate||'');
1890:   const year=date.slice(0,4),month=Number(date.slice(5,7));
1891:   if(year==='2026')return month&&month<=7?'g1s1':'g1s2';
1892:   if(year==='2027')return month&&month<=7?'g2s1':'g2s2';
1893:   if(year==='2028')return 'g3';
1894:   return '';
1895: }
1896: function dormProgramIsHolisticTarget(program){
1897:   return program?.kind==='selected'||(program?.kind==='common'&&program?.reportRequired===true);
1898: }
1899: function dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode){
1900:   const view=studentActivityView(student);
1901:   const order=['g1s1','g1s2','g2s1','g2s2','g3'];
1902:   const cutoff=Math.max(0,order.indexOf(termMode));
1903:   const allowed=new Set(order.slice(0,cutoff+1));
1904:   const programs=(view.holisticPrograms||[]).filter(p=>allowed.has(dormProgramTermKey(p)));
1905:   const canonical=studentCanonicalReports(student);
1906:   const reportTargets=programs.filter(p=>programRequiresStudentReport(p,student,canonical));
1907:   const reports=[...new Map(reportTargets.map(p=>resolveStudentProgramReport(p,student,canonical)).filter(Boolean).map(r=>[canonicalReportKey(r),r])).values()];
1908:   return {programs,reportTargets,reports,submitted:reports.length,reportRate:reportTargets.length?Math.min(100,reports.length/reportTargets.length*100):0};
1909: }
1910: function dormCohortAverage(termMode){
1911:   const roster=dormTermRoster(termMode);
1912:   const stats=roster.map(row=>{
1913:     const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.id)===String(row.studentId)||String(s.studentNo)===String(row.studentNo));
1914:     return student?dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode):null;
1915:   }).filter(Boolean);
1916:   const avg=(key)=>stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+Number(x[key]||0),0)/stats.length:0;
1917:   return {count:stats.length,programs:stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.programs.length,0)/stats.length:0,reportTargets:stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+(x.reportTargets?.length||0),0)/stats.length:0,submitted:avg('submitted'),reportRate:avg('reportRate')};
1918: }

1932: }
1933: function dormInseoulGap(student){
1934:   const row=admissionManagementDataset('inseoul').find(x=>String(x.student?.id||'')===String(student.id||''));
1935:   const standard=row?.standard||{internal:uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal,mockText:uepManagementRule('inseoul').mockText};
1936:   const actual=row?.base?.actual9;
1937:   const internalGap=actual==null?null:Math.max(0,(actual-Number(standard.internal))/Number(standard.internal)*100);
1938:   const levels=(row?.mock?.mainLevels||[]).filter(Number.isFinite);
1939:   const within3=levels.filter(v=>v<=3).length,within2=levels.filter(v=>v<=2).length;
1940:   const mockScore=levels.length?Math.min(100,((Math.min(within3,3)+Math.min(within2,2))/5)*100):null;
1941:   const mockGap=mockScore==null?null:100-mockScore;
1942:   const overall=[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null).length?Math.max(...[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null)):null;
1943:   const gaps=[];
1944:   if(actual==null)gaps.push('내신 9등급 환산자료 확인 필요');
1945:   else if(actual>standard.internal)gaps.push(`내신 ${actual.toFixed(2)} → 핵심기준 ${Number(standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내 보완`);
1946:   else gaps.push(`내신 핵심기준 충족 (${actual.toFixed(2)})`);
1947:   if(levels.length<3)gaps.push('국·수·영 모의고사 자료 보완');
1948:   else{
1949:     levels.forEach((v,i)=>{if(v>3)gaps.push(`${['국어','수학','영어'][i]||'주요영역'} ${v}등급 → 3등급 이내`);});
1950:     if(within2<2)gaps.push(`국·수·영 2등급 이내 ${within2}개 → 2개 필요`);
1951:     if(within3===3&&within2>=2)gaps.push('모의고사 인서울 핵심기준 충족');
1952:   }
1953:   return {row,standard,actual,internalGap,mockGap,overall,gaps};
1954: }
1955: function dormComprehensiveCounselMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
1956:   const gap=dormInseoulGap(student),career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle),stats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);
1957:   const selected=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N');
1958:   const points=[...gap.gaps];
1959:   if(!selected.length)points.push('희망계열에 맞는 선택과목 이수계획 확인 필요');
1960:   if(stats.programs.length<cohort.programs)points.push(`계열 연계 프로그램: 본인 ${stats.programs.length}개 / 학사생 평균 ${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}개`);
1961:   if(stats.reportRate+1<cohort.reportRate)points.push(`보고서 제출률: 본인 ${Math.round(stats.reportRate)}% / 학사생 평균 ${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%`);
1962:   points.push(`진로희망 ${career.hopeTrack} · ${career.hopeMajor}와 선택과목·탐구활동의 연속성 확인`);
1963:   return `<section class="dorm-counsel-priority"><header><div><small>SEOUL · CORE GAP</small><h4>인서울 핵심관리 괴리 최우선</h4></div><strong>${gap.overall==null?'자료 확인':gap.overall<=0?'핵심기준 충족':`최대 괴리 ${gap.overall.toFixed(0)}%`}</strong></header><div class="dorm-gap-metrics"><span><small>내신 핵심기준</small><b>9등급 ${Number(gap.standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내</b></span><span><small>현재 내신</small><b>${gap.actual==null?'-':gap.actual.toFixed(2)}</b><em>${gap.internalGap==null?'자료 없음':gap.internalGap===0?'충족':`괴리 +${gap.internalGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</em></span><span><small>모의 핵심기준</small><b>국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3</b></span><span><small>모의 충족 괴리</small><b>${gap.mockGap==null?'-':`${gap.mockGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</b></span></div><ol>${points.map(p=>`<li>${escapeHtml(p)}</li>`).join('')}</ol><p class="dorm-holistic-note"><b>학생부종합전형 관점</b> 성적만으로 순위를 매기지 않고 진로희망–선택과목–계열연계 프로그램–보고서 탐구의 연결성과 지속성을 함께 상담합니다.</p></section>`;
1964: }

1966: function dormSeoulUniversityRows(student){
1967:   const seoul=/서울대|연세대|고려대|서강대|성균관대|한양대|중앙대|경희대|한국외대|서울시립대|건국대|동국대|홍익대|숙명여대|이화여대|광운대|국민대|숭실대|세종대|서울과기대/;
1968:   const base=admissionBase(student.id), actual=base.actual9;
1969:   const cuts=(readonlyCache?.admissionCuts||[]).filter(x=>seoul.test(String(x.university||''))&&Number.isFinite(Number(x.cut)));
1970:   const grouped=new Map();
1971:   cuts.forEach(row=>{const key=String(row.university||'').trim();if(!key)return;const diff=actual==null?null:actual-Number(row.cut);const old=grouped.get(key);if(!old||Math.abs(diff??99)<Math.abs(old.diff??99))grouped.set(key,{...row,diff});});
1972:   return [...grouped.values()].sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(a.diff??99)-Math.abs(b.diff??99)).slice(0,9);
1973: }

2106: function programStudentGrade(student) {

2614: function inputCenterStudentMatch(textValue='',fileName=''){
2615:   const students=inputCenterBatchStudents();
2616:   const hay=`${fileName||''}\n${textValue||''}`;
2617:   const numbers=[...hay.matchAll(/(?:^|[^0-9])([123][0-9]{3})(?![0-9])/g)].map(m=>m[1]);
2618:   for(const no of numbers){const student=students.find(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===no);if(student)return {student,matchStatus:'자동매칭'};}
2619:   const shortHay=hay.slice(0,4000);
2620:   const matched=students.filter(s=>s.name&&shortHay.includes(String(s.name)));
2621:   if(matched.length===1)return {student:matched[0],matchStatus:'이름매칭'};
2622:   return {student:null,matchStatus:'확인필요'};
2623: }

2628: }

2874: async function inputCenterRunAutoOCR(drafts){

2878:   const byKey=new Map((result.results||[]).map(x=>[x.key,x]));
2879:   for(const d of drafts){const pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];const texts=[],engines=new Set(),errors=[];pages.forEach((p,i)=>{const r=byKey.get(`${d.draftId}::${i}`);if(!r)return;if(r.ok&&r.text)texts.push(`[${i+1}페이지]\n${r.text}`);if(r.engine)engines.add(r.engine);if(!r.ok)errors.push(`${i+1}p ${r.reason||r.status||'OCR 실패'}`);});d.text=texts.join('\n\n').trim();d.rawText=d.text;d.ocrEngine=[...engines].join(' + ');d.ocrError=errors.join(' / ');d.ocrStatus=errors.length?(d.text?'OCR확인필요':'OCR오류'):(d.text?'OCR완료':'OCR확인필요');inputCenterApplyOcrMatch(d);}

3016: function submissionDateText(value){
3017:   const raw=String(value??"").trim();
3018:   if(!raw)return "-";
3019:   const serial=Number(raw);
3020:   let date=null;
3021:   if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)date=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000);
3022:   else{
3023:     const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));
3024:     if(Number.isFinite(parsed))date=new Date(parsed);
3025:   }
3026:   if(!date||Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))return raw;
3027:   return `${date.getFullYear()}.${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}.${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
3028: }

3932: function accessAccountRole(account){
3933:   if(String(account?.admin||'').toUpperCase()==='Y')return '관리자';
3934:   const role=String(account?.role||'').trim();
3935:   if(/학년부장/.test(role))return '학년부장';
3936:   if(/학년총무/.test(role))return '학년총무';
3937:   if(/담임/.test(role))return '담임교사';
3938:   if(/교감/.test(role))return '교감';
3939:   if(/교장/.test(role))return '교장';
3940:   return '교과담당';
3941: }

4108: function scoreTrend(row, allRows) {
4109:   const orders = row.scoreType === "모의고사" ? ["3월모의고사","6월모의고사","9월모의고사","10월모의고사","11월모의고사"] : ["1차고사","2차고사","학기말고사"];
4110:   const index = orders.indexOf(row.exam);
4111:   if (index <= 0 || row.level === "" || row.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
4112:   const period = scorePeriodKey(row);
4113:   const previous = allRows.find(x => x.studentId === row.studentId && x.scoreType === row.scoreType && x.subject === row.subject && x.exam === orders[index - 1] && scorePeriodKey(x) === period);
4114:   if (!previous || previous.level === "" || previous.level == null) return { text:"-", cls:"" };
4115:   const diff = Number(previous.level) - Number(row.level);
4116:   if (!Number.isFinite(diff) || diff === 0) return { text:"유지", cls:"" };
4117:   return diff > 0 ? { text:`▲ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"good" } : { text:`▼ ${Math.abs(diff)}등급`, cls:"bad" };
4118: }

6584: function uniqueActiveAlertStudents(rows=[]){
6585:   const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
6586:   const validIds=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.id||s.studentId||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
6587:   const validNos=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.studentNo||s.number||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
6588:   const seen=new Set(),out=[];
6589:   for(const row of rows){
6590:     const id=String(row?.studentId||row?.id||'').trim(),no=String(row?.studentNo||row?.number||'').trim();
6591:     const hasKnownIdentity=(id&&validIds.has(id))||(no&&validNos.has(no));
6592:     if((validIds.size||validNos.size)&&!hasKnownIdentity)continue;
6593:     const key=(id&&validIds.has(id)?id:'')||(no&&validNos.has(no)?no:'')||`${row?.classNo||row?.classLabel||''}|${row?.name||''}`;
6594:     if(!key||seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);out.push(row);
6595:   }
6596:   return out;
6597: }

6790: function downloadWordDraft(fileName, title, body) {
6791:   const html = `<!doctype html><html lang="ko"><meta charset="utf-8"><style>body{font-family:'Malgun Gothic',sans-serif;margin:45px;line-height:1.8}h1{text-align:center;font-size:22px}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:25px 0}td{border:1px solid #777;padding:8px}pre{font:14px/1.8 'Malgun Gothic',sans-serif;white-space:pre-wrap}</style><h1>${escapeHtml(title)}</h1>${body}`;
6792:   const blob = new Blob(["\ufeff", html], { type: "application/msword;charset=utf-8" });
6793:   const link = document.createElement("a");
6794:   link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
6795:   link.download = `${fileName.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, "_")}.doc`;
6796:   link.click();
6797:   setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href), 1000);
6798: }

7117: function schoolPeriodStatus(now) {
7118:   const minute = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
7119:   const periods = [[520,570],[580,630],[640,690],[700,750],[820,870],[880,930],[940,990]];
7120:   const index = periods.findIndex(([start, end]) => minute >= start && minute <= end);
7121:   if (index >= 0) return { index, label: `현재 ${index + 1}교시` };
7122:   if (minute < periods[0][0]) return { index: -1, label: "일과 시작 전" };
7123:   if (minute > periods[6][1]) return { index: -1, label: "정규수업 종료" };
7124:   if (minute >= 750 && minute < 820) return { index: -1, label: "점심시간" };
7125:   return { index: -1, label: "쉬는 시간" };
7126: }

7473: function timetableClassColorKey(room){
7474:   const match=String(room||"").match(/(?:^|\b)([123])-?(\d{1,2})(?:\b|$)/);
7475:   if(!match)return "";
7476:   return `${match[1]}-${Number(match[2])}`;
7477: }
7478: function timetableClassColorClass(room){
7479:   const key=timetableClassColorKey(room);
7480:   if(!key)return "";
7481:   let hash=0; for(const ch of key)hash=(hash*31+ch.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;
7482:   return `class-color-${hash%9}`;
7483: }

8393: function normalizeTimetableGradeClass(row){
8394:   const directGrade=Number(row?.grade??row?.학년??0);
8395:   const directClass=Number(row?.classNo??row?.class??row?.반??0);
8396:   if(directGrade>=1&&directGrade<=3&&directClass>=1&&directClass<=20)return {grade:directGrade,classNo:directClass};
8397:   const raw=String(row?.classCode??row?.classroom??row?.학급??row?.반코드??row?.['학년반']??'').trim();
8398:   const digits=raw.replace(/\D/g,'');
8399:   if(digits.length===3){
8400:     const grade=Number(digits[0]),classNo=Number(digits.slice(1));
8401:     if(grade>=1&&grade<=3&&classNo>=1&&classNo<=20)return {grade,classNo};
8402:   }
8403:   if(digits.length===4){
8404:     const grade=Number(digits[0]),classNo=Number(digits.slice(1,3));
8405:     if(grade>=1&&grade<=3&&classNo>=1&&classNo<=20)return {grade,classNo};
8406:   }
8407:   return {grade:directGrade||1,classNo:directClass||Number(digits)||0};
8408: }

8621: function showApprovalDetail(index) {
8622:   const row = SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA.approval[Number(index)] || SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA.approval[0];

8624:   if (!target || !row) return;
8625:   target.innerHTML = `<small>업무 분류</small><h3>${escapeHtml(row[0])}</h3><section><b>결재 순서</b><p>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "-")}</p></section><section><b>확인 사항</b><p>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "별도 비고 없음")}</p></section><footer>2026년 학교 주요 업무 결재 라인 기준 · 읽기 전용</footer>`;
8626:   $$(".approval-nav-row").forEach((button) => button.classList.toggle("active", Number(button.dataset.approvalIndex) === Number(index)));

8629: function bindExtensionReference() {
8630:   const imageButton = $("[data-extension-tab='image']");
8631:   const tableButton = $("[data-extension-tab='table']");
8632:   const imagePanel = $("#extensionImagePanel");
8633:   const tablePanel = $("#extensionTablePanel");
8634:   const switchTab = (name) => {
8635:     imageButton?.classList.toggle("active", name === "image");
8636:     tableButton?.classList.toggle("active", name === "table");
8637:     imagePanel?.classList.toggle("hidden", name !== "image");
8638:     tablePanel?.classList.toggle("hidden", name !== "table");
8639:   };
8640:   if (imageButton) imageButton.onclick = () => switchTab("image");
8641:   if (tableButton) tableButton.onclick = () => switchTab("table");
8642:   const image = $("#extensionLayoutImage");
8643:   if (image) image.onclick = () => { const zoomed = image.classList.toggle("zoomed"); image.closest(".extension-image-scroll")?.classList.toggle("zoom-mode", zoomed); };
8644:   const search = $("#sourceReferenceSearch");
8645:   if (search) search.oninput = () => {
8646:     const query = search.value.trim().toLowerCase();
8647:     const filtered = SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA.extension.filter((row) => row.join(" ").toLowerCase().includes(query));
8648:     $("#sourceReferenceRows").innerHTML = sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(filtered, "extension");
8649:   };
8650: }

8652: function teacherReferenceInfo(name){
8653:   const teacher=String(name||"").trim();
8654:   const extension=(SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA.extension||[]).find(row=>String(row?.[1]||"").replace(/[()]/g,"").includes(teacher));
8655:   const duties=(DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS||[]).filter(item=>String(item?.person||"").trim()===teacher);
8656:   const personal=String(state.settings?.teacherPersonalContacts?.[teacher]||"").trim();
8657:   return {name:teacher,office:String(extension?.[0]||"-"),extension:String(extension?.[2]||"").trim()||"-",directPhone:String(extension?.[3]||"").trim()||"-",personal:personal||"연결 정보 없음",duties};
8658: }

8670: function openSourceReference(type) {
8671:   const meta = {
8672:     approval: ["APPROVAL LINE", "주요 업무 결재라인"],
8673:     homeroom: ["HOMEROOM", "담임·부담임 명단"],
8674:     extension: ["DIRECTORY & SEATING", "내선번호·자리배치"],
8675:   }[type];
8676:   const rows = SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA[type] || [];
8677:   $("#drawerKicker").textContent = meta[0];
8678:   $("#drawerTitle").textContent = meta[1];
8679:   $("#drawer").classList.remove("reference-expanded", "reference-maximized", "extension-expanded");
8680:   if (type === "approval") {
8681:     $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="reference-editor-tools source-reference-tools"><input id="sourceReferenceSearch" placeholder="업무 종류·결재선 검색"><span>${rows.length}건</span></div><div class="approval-explorer"><div id="approvalList" class="approval-nav">${approvalListMarkup(rows)}</div><article id="approvalDetail" class="approval-detail"></article></div>`;
8682:     $("#drawer").classList.add("reference-maximized");
8683:     $("#sourceReferenceSearch").oninput = () => {
8684:       const query = $("#sourceReferenceSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
8685:       const filtered = rows.filter((row) => row.join(" ").toLowerCase().includes(query));
8686:       $("#approvalList").innerHTML = approvalListMarkup(filtered);
8687:       $("#approvalList").querySelectorAll("[data-approval-index]").forEach((button) => button.onclick = () => showApprovalDetail(button.dataset.approvalIndex));
8688:       if (filtered.length) showApprovalDetail(SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA.approval.indexOf(filtered[0]));
8689:     };
8690:     $("#approvalList").querySelectorAll("[data-approval-index]").forEach((button) => button.onclick = () => showApprovalDetail(button.dataset.approvalIndex));
8691:     showApprovalDetail(0);
8692:   } else if (type === "extension") {
8693:     $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<section id="extensionImagePanel" class="extension-image-panel extension-image-only"><div class="extension-image-scroll"><img id="extensionLayoutImage" src="./assets/extension-layout.png" alt="운호고등학교 인터넷전화 내선번호 자리배치도"></div></section>`;
8694:     $("#drawer").classList.add("extension-expanded");
8695:     bindExtensionReference();
8696:   } else {
8697:     $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="reference-editor-tools source-reference-tools"><input id="sourceReferenceSearch" placeholder="학년·반·담임·부담임 검색"><span>${rows.length}건</span></div><div id="sourceReferenceRows">${sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(rows, type)}</div><p class="drawer-note">2026년 담임교사 명단(부담임 포함) 기준 · 읽기 전용</p>`;
8698:     $("#drawer").classList.add("reference-expanded");
8699:     const bindHomeroomIfNeeded=()=>{if(type==="homeroom")bindHomeroomTeacherLinks();};
8700:     bindHomeroomIfNeeded();
8701:     $("#sourceReferenceSearch").oninput = () => {
8702:       const query = $("#sourceReferenceSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
8703:       const filtered = rows.filter((row) => row.join(" ").toLowerCase().includes(query));
8704:       $("#sourceReferenceRows").innerHTML = sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(filtered, type);
8705:       bindHomeroomIfNeeded();
8706:     };
8707:   }
8708:   $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
8709:   $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
8710: }
8711: 
8712: function sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(rows, type) {
8713:   if (!rows.length) return '<div class="outing-empty">검색 결과가 없습니다.</div>';
8714:   if (type === "homeroom") {
8715:     const grades = [...new Set(rows.map((row) => String(row[0])))].sort();
8716:     const gradeHeads = { "1": "홍석민", "2": "윤용철", "3": "정조일" };
8717:     return `<div class="homeroom-grade-sections">${grades.map((grade) => {
8718:       const gradeRows = rows.filter((row) => String(row[0]) === grade);
8719:       return `<section class="homeroom-grade-section"><header><div><span>${escapeHtml(grade)}학년</span><b>학년부장 ${escapeHtml(gradeHeads[grade] || "-")}</b></div><small>${gradeRows.length}개 학급</small></header><div class="source-homeroom-grid">${gradeRows.map((row)=>`<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[1])}반</small><button class="homeroom-teacher-link primary" data-homeroom-teacher="${escapeHtml(row[2])}">${escapeHtml(row[2])}</button><span>부담임 <button class="homeroom-teacher-link" data-homeroom-teacher="${escapeHtml(row[3])}">${escapeHtml(row[3])}</button></span></div>`).join("")}</div></section>`;
8720:     }).join("")}</div>`;
8721:   }
8722:   if (type === "extension") {
8723:     return `<div class="source-extension-table"><div class="source-reference-head"><b>구역</b><b>이름·시설</b><b>내선</b><b>전화번호</b></div>${rows.map((row)=>`<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[0] || "-")}</small><b>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "-")}</b><em>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "-")}</em><span>${escapeHtml(row[3] || "-")}</span></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
8724:   }
8725:   return `<div class="source-approval-list">${rows.map((row)=>`<div><b>${escapeHtml(row[0])}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "")}</span>${row[2] ? `<small>${escapeHtml(row[2])}</small>` : ""}</div>`).join("")}</div>`;
8726: }
8727: 
8728: function openEditableReference(type) {
8729:   const meta = {
8730:     approval: ["APPROVAL LINE", "주요 업무 결재라인", ["업무분류", "결재라인", "비고"]],
8731:     homeroom: ["HOMEROOM", "담임교사 명단", ["학년", "반", "담임", "부담임"]],
8732:     extension: ["DIRECTORY", "내선번호·자리배치", ["구역", "이름·시설", "자리·대표번호", "내선"]],
8733:   }[type];
8734:   const rows = state.settings.referenceData?.[type] || [];
8735:   $("#drawerKicker").textContent = meta[0];

8764: function referenceRowsMarkup(rows, type = "") {
8765:   if (!rows.length) return '<div class="reference-detail"><div class="outing-empty">등록된 자료가 없습니다. 표 전체 편집에서 원본 표를 붙여넣으세요.</div></div>';
8766:   if (type === "homeroom") {
8767:     const grades = [...new Set(rows.map((row) => String(row[0])))].sort();
8768:     return `<div class="homeroom-grade-sections">${grades.map((grade) => {
8769:       const gradeRows = rows.filter((row) => String(row[0]) === grade);
8770:       return `<section class="homeroom-grade-section"><header><span>${escapeHtml(grade)}학년</span><small>${gradeRows.length}개 학급</small></header><div class="reference-table reference-homeroom">${gradeRows.map((row) => `<div><b>${escapeHtml(row[1])}반</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "-")}</span><small>부담임 ${escapeHtml(row[3] || "-")}</small></div>`).join("")}</div></section>`;
8771:     }).join("")}</div>`;
8772:   }
8773:   if (type === "extension") {
8774:     return `<div class="reference-table reference-extension">${rows.map((row) => `<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[0] || "기타")}</small><b>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "-")}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "")}</span><em>${escapeHtml(row[3] || "")}</em></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
8775:   }
8776:   return `<div class="reference-detail">${rows.map((row) => `<div class="reference-row reference-approval"><b>${escapeHtml(row[0])}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "")}</span>${row[2] ? `<small>${escapeHtml(row[2])}</small>` : ""}</div>`).join("")}</div>`;
8777: }
8778: 
8779: function parseReferencePaste(text, type) {
8780:   const headers = {
8781:     approval: ["업무분류", "업무 종류", "결재라인", "비고"],
8782:     homeroom: ["학년", "반", "담임", "부담임"],
8783:     extension: ["구역", "이름", "시설", "자리", "대표번호", "내선"],
8784:   }[type] || [];
8785:   const widths = { approval: 3, homeroom: 4, extension: 4 };
8786:   return String(text || "").split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => {
8787:     const cells = (line.includes("\t") ? line.split("\t") : line.split(/\s{2,}|,/))
8788:       .map((cell) => String(cell || "").trim());
8789:     while (cells.length && !cells[cells.length - 1]) cells.pop();
8790:     return cells.slice(0, widths[type] || cells.length);
8791:   }).filter((row) => {
8792:     const joined = row.join(" ");
8793:     return row.filter(Boolean).length >= 2 && !headers.some((header) => joined === header || joined.startsWith(`${header} `));
8794:   });
8795: }

9230: function studentAccessContext(student){
9231:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
9232:   const studentClass=String(student?.className||student?.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
9233:   const ownClass=String(scope.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
9234:   const role=currentRoleId();
9235:   const isAdmin=role==="admin";
9236:   const isOwnHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&ownClass&&studentClass&&String(Number(ownClass))===String(Number(studentClass));
9237:   const isOtherHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&!isOwnHomeroom;
9238:   const isReadOnlyLeadership=role==="vice_principal"||role==="principal";
9239:   return {role,isAdmin,isOwnHomeroom,isOtherHomeroom,isReadOnlyLeadership,canEditContact:!isReadOnlyLeadership&&(isAdmin||isOwnHomeroom)};
9240: }
9241: function sensitiveRevealKey(student,card){return `${student?.id||student?.studentNo||"unknown"}:${card}`;}
9242: function isStudentSensitiveCardMasked(student,card){
9243:   const access=studentAccessContext(student);
9244:   // 성적·입시는 상담/진학 협업을 위해 타반도 조회하되 UEP에서는 수정하지 않는다.
9245:   if(card==="score") return false;
9246:   // 연락처·이메일·보호자 연락처는 타반 담임에게 기본 마스킹하고 필요 시 현재 화면에서만 일시 공개한다.
9247:   return card==="contact"&&(access.isOtherHomeroom||access.isReadOnlyLeadership)&&!studentSensitiveRevealKeys.has(sensitiveRevealKey(student,card));
9248: }

9872: function selectStudentDashboardById(studentId){
9873:   studentSensitiveRevealKeys.clear();
9874:   const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
9875:   const key=String(studentId||'').trim();
9876:   const idx=students.findIndex(student=>String(student.id||'')===key || String(student.studentNo||'')===key);
9877:   if(idx<0)return false;
9878:   studentDashboardIndex=idx;
9879:   selectedStudentId=students[idx].id||students[idx].studentNo||"";
9880:   studentPageMode='dashboard';
9881:   window.__uepStudentDashboardSwitchToken=(window.__uepStudentDashboardSwitchToken||0)+1;
9882:   return true;
9883: }
9884: function switchStudentDashboard(studentId){

9890:   window.scrollTo(0,0);
9891:   return true;
9892: }

10098:     const input = $(inputSelector);
10099:     const reset = $(resetSelector);
10100:     const run = () => {
10101:       const query = (input?.value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
10102:       $$(rowSelector).forEach((row) => {
10103:         const matchesText = !query || String(row.dataset.search || "").includes(query);
10104:         const matchesExtra = extraFilter ? extraFilter(row) : true;
10105:         row.classList.toggle("hidden", !(matchesText && matchesExtra));
10106:       });
10107:     };
10108:     if (input) input.oninput = run;
10109:     if (reset) reset.onclick = () => {
10110:       if (input) input.value = "";
10111:       run();
10112:     };
10113:     return run;

10713: function bindQuickLinkSorting() {
10714:   const container = $(".quick-links");
10715:   if (!container) return;
10716:   let timer = null;
10717:   let active = null;
10718:   const finish = async () => {
10719:     clearTimeout(timer);
10720:     timer = null;
10721:     if (!active) return;
10722:     active.classList.remove("quick-dragging");
10723:     container.classList.remove("quick-sorting");
10724:     active = null;
10725:     state.settings.quickOrder = [...container.querySelectorAll("button")].map((button) => button.dataset.reference);
10726:     await save();
10727:     toast("빠른 열기 순서를 저장했습니다.");
10728:   };
10729:   container.querySelectorAll("button").forEach((button) => {
10730:     button.onpointerdown = (event) => {
10731:       if (event.pointerType === "mouse" && event.button !== 0) return;
10732:       timer = setTimeout(() => {
10733:         active = button;
10734:         button.dataset.suppressClick = "1";
10735:         button.classList.add("quick-dragging");
10736:         container.classList.add("quick-sorting");
10737:         button.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
10738:         toast("이제 끌어서 위치를 바꾸세요.");
10739:       }, 500);
10740:     };
10741:     button.onpointermove = (event) => {
10742:       if (!active) return;
10743:       event.preventDefault();
10744:       const target = document.elementFromPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY)?.closest(".quick-links button");
10745:       if (!target || target === active || target.parentElement !== container) return;
10746:       const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
10747:       const after = event.clientY > rect.top + rect.height / 2 || event.clientX > rect.left + rect.width / 2;
10748:       container.insertBefore(active, after ? target.nextSibling : target);
10749:     };
10750:     button.onpointerup = finish;
10751:     button.onpointercancel = finish;
10752:     button.onpointerleave = () => {
10753:       if (!active) clearTimeout(timer);
10754:     };
10755:   });
10756: }

11405: function openDormOutingDrawer() {
11406:   const viewKey = dateKey(outingViewDate);
11407:   const liveOutings = (readonlyCache?.dormOutings || []).filter(item=>!dormOutingExcludedByRule(item));
11408:   const items = liveOutings.filter((item) => item.date === viewKey);
11409:   const monday = new Date(outingViewDate);
11410:   const day = monday.getDay();
11411:   monday.setDate(monday.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
11412:   const sunday = new Date(monday);
11413:   sunday.setDate(sunday.getDate() + 6);
11414:   const weekItems = liveOutings.filter((item) => item.date >= dateKey(monday) && item.date <= dateKey(sunday));
11415:   const displayDate = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("ko-KR", { year: "numeric", month: "long", day: "numeric", weekday: "short" }).format(outingViewDate);
11416:   $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "DORMITORY · OUTING";
11417:   $("#drawerTitle").textContent = `학사외출 · ${displayDate}`;
11418:   const outingMasterStudent = (item) => {
11419:     const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
11420:     const ids=[item.studentId,item.studentNo,item.studentNumber].filter(Boolean).map(String);
11421:     const byId=students.find(student=>ids.some(id=>id===String(student.id||"")||id===String(student.studentId||"")||id===String(student.studentNo||"")));
11422:     if(byId)return byId;
11423:     const name=String(item.name||"").trim();
11424:     const cls=String(item.className||item.class||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
11425:     const no=String(item.number||item.no||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
11426:     return students.find(student=>String(student.name||"").trim()===name && (!cls||String(student.className||"").replace(/\D/g,"")===cls) && (!no||String(student.number||"").replace(/\D/g,"")===no)) || null;
11427:   };
11428:   const outingStudentNo = (item) => {
11429:     const master=outingMasterStudent(item);
11430:     const masterNo=String(master?.studentNo||"").replace(/\.0$/,"").trim();
11431:     if (/^[123]\d{3}$/.test(masterNo)) return masterNo;
11432:     const direct = String(item.studentNo || item.studentNumber || "").replace(/\.0$/,"").trim();
11433:     if (/^[123]\d{3}$/.test(direct)) return direct;
11434:     const classDigits = String(master?.className||item.className || item.class || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
11435:     const numberDigits = String(master?.number||item.number || item.no || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
11436:     if (!classDigits || !numberDigits) return "-";
11437:     return `${classDigits}${numberDigits.padStart(2, "0")}`;
11438:   };
11439:   const outingEmail=(item)=>String(item.email||item.studentEmail||outingMasterStudent(item)?.email||"").trim();
11440:   const rows = (list) => list.length ? list.map((item) => `<div class="outing-row"><time>${item.date.slice(5).replace("-", "/")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(outingStudentNo(item))} ${escapeHtml(item.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.className)}반 ${escapeHtml(item.number)}번 · ${dormOutingIsMedical(item)?'<b class="state positive">병원</b> ':''}${escapeHtml(item.category || "외출")}</small></span><time>${escapeHtml(item.outTime || "-")}</time><time>${escapeHtml(item.returnTime || "-")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(item.reason || "-")}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.destination || "-")}</small></span><span class="state">${escapeHtml(item.status || "예정")}</span></div>`).join("") : `<div class="outing-empty">표시할 학사외출 기록이 없습니다.</div>`;
11441:   const weekDays = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, index) => {
11442:     const date = new Date(monday);
11443:     date.setDate(date.getDate() + index);
11444:     const key = dateKey(date);
11445:     const list = weekItems.filter((item) => item.date === key);
11446:     return `<section><header><b>${["월", "화", "수", "목", "금","토","일"][index]}</b><span>${key.slice(5).replace("-", "/")}</span><em>${list.length}명</em></header>${list.length ? list.map((item) => `<div class="outing-week-item"><time>${escapeHtml(item.outTime || "-")}–${escapeHtml(item.returnTime || "-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(item.name)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(item.className)}반 ${escapeHtml(item.number)}번 · ${dormOutingIsMedical(item)?'<b class="state positive">병원</b> ':''}${escapeHtml(item.category || "외출")}</small><span>${escapeHtml(item.reason || "-")}</span></div>`).join("") : "<p>신청 없음</p>"}</section>`;
11447:   }).join("");
11448:   const smsText=(item)=>{const late=/늦은 입소/.test(item.category||'');return late?`${item.name} 학생에게 안내합니다.\n[운호고등학교 학사 입소 안내]\n${displayDate} 늦은 입소 신청이 확인되었습니다.\n도착 예정: ${item.returnTime||"-"}\n확인정보: ${[item.reason,item.destination].filter(Boolean).join(" / ")||"-"}\n신청한 입소시간을 준수해 주세요.`:`${item.name} 학생에게 안내합니다.\n[운호고등학교 학사외출 안내]\n${displayDate} 학사외출이 확인되었습니다.\n외출: ${item.outTime||"-"} / 복귀·귀가: ${item.returnTime||"-"}\n확인정보: ${[item.reason,item.destination].filter(Boolean).join(" / ")||"-"}\n복귀시간을 반드시 준수하고, 복귀 후 담당 선생님께 확인받기 바랍니다.`};

12305: function uepCompareSelectionHistory(rows=[]){

12317:       const pre=new Set(semRows.filter(r=>/사전|상담|구글폼|google/i.test(uepSelectionRound(r)+' '+String(r['원본시트']??''))).map(uepSelectionNormalizeCourse).filter(Boolean));
12318:       const main=new Set(semRows.filter(r=>/본신청|리로|수강신청/i.test(uepSelectionRound(r)+' '+String(r['원본시트']??''))).map(uepSelectionNormalizeCourse).filter(Boolean));

12322:       const added=[...main].filter(x=>!pre.has(x));

12325:     }

12327:   return out;

967: function dormRuleDateValue(value){
968:   if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return '';
969:   const raw=String(value).trim();
970:   if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){
971:     const serial=Number(raw);
972:     if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
973:       const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
974:       if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
975:         const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),mo=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
976:         const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
977:         return `${y}-${mo}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
978:       }
979:     }
980:   }
981:   const mm=raw.replace(' ','T').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
982:   return mm?`${mm[1]}-${String(mm[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(mm[3]).padStart(2,'0')}T${String(mm[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${mm[5]}`:raw;
983: }

57: function normalizeHeaderText(value) {
58:   return String(value ?? "")
59:     .replace(/\r?\n/g, " ")
60:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
61:     .trim();
62: }

64: function detectHeaderRowIndex(rows) {
65:   const source = Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [];
66:   const scanLimit = Math.min(source.length, 12);
67:   let bestIndex = -1;
68:   let bestScore = -Infinity;
69: 
70:   for (let index = 0; index < scanLimit; index += 1) {
71:     const cells = (source[index] || []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
72:     const nonEmpty = cells.filter(Boolean);
73:     if (nonEmpty.length < 2) continue;
74: 
75:     const uniqueCount = new Set(nonEmpty).size;
76:     const duplicatePenalty = nonEmpty.length - uniqueCount;
77:     const knownHeaderHits = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>
78:       /^(학생ID|학번|성명|사용성명|학년|반|번호|일자|운영일|일정ID|프로그램ID|강좌ID|기록ID|상태|구분|설정키|파일구분|스프레드시트ID|탭명|탭용도)$/.test(value),
79:     ).length;
80:     const titlePenalty = nonEmpty.length <= 2 && /UEP|표준|시트|DB|관리|기준/.test(nonEmpty.join(" ")) ? 8 : 0;
81:     const score = nonEmpty.length * 3 + uniqueCount + knownHeaderHits * 8 - duplicatePenalty * 2 - titlePenalty;
82: 
83:     if (score > bestScore) {
84:       bestScore = score;
85:       bestIndex = index;
86:     }
87:   }
88: 
89:   return bestIndex;
90: }

92: function headerLookupKey(value) {
93:   return normalizeHeaderText(value).replace(/[\s·_\-()\[\]{}]/g, "").toLowerCase();
94: }

96: function valueByHeader(row, ...aliases) {
97:   if (!row || typeof row !== "object") return "";
98:   for (const alias of aliases) {
99:     if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(row, alias)) return row[alias];
100:   }
101:   const wanted = new Set(aliases.map(headerLookupKey));
102:   for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(row)) {
103:     const normalizedKey = headerLookupKey(key);
104:     if (wanted.has(normalizedKey)) return value;
105:     // 병합형 2단 헤더(예: "학생정보 학번", "외출정보 외출일자")도
106:     // 하위 헤더 별칭으로 안전하게 찾습니다.
107:     for (const aliasKey of wanted) {
108:       if (aliasKey && normalizedKey.endsWith(aliasKey)) return value;
109:     }
110:   }
111:   return "";
112: }

114: function looksLikeSubHeaderRow(row) {
115:   const cells = (Array.isArray(row) ? row : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
116:   const nonEmpty = cells.filter(Boolean);
117:   if (nonEmpty.length < 2) return false;
118:   const headerTerms = nonEmpty.filter((value) =>
119:     /^(?:원점수|표준점수|백분위|등급|과목|학생ID|학번|성명|이름|시험명|시험월|시험년도|학년도|반|번호|일자|날짜|외출일자|외출시간|복귀예정시간|귀사예정시간|자택도착예정시간|사유|목적지|구분|상태|처리상태|승인자|비고)$/.test(value),
120:   ).length;
121:   const numericLike = nonEmpty.filter((value) => /^[-+]?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?$/.test(value)).length;
122:   return headerTerms >= 2 || (headerTerms >= 1 && numericLike === 0);
123: }

125: function composeTwoLevelHeaders(parentRow, childRow) {
126:   const parents = (Array.isArray(parentRow) ? parentRow : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
127:   const children = (Array.isArray(childRow) ? childRow : []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
128:   const width = Math.max(parents.length, children.length);
129:   const result = [];
130:   let activeParent = "";
131:   for (let index = 0; index < width; index += 1) {
132:     const parent = parents[index] || "";
133:     const child = children[index] || "";
134:     if (parent) activeParent = parent;
135:     if (child) {
136:       const parentKey = headerLookupKey(activeParent);
137:       const childKey = headerLookupKey(child);
138:       result.push(parentKey && parentKey !== childKey ? `${activeParent} ${child}`.trim() : child);
139:     } else {
140:       result.push(parent || activeParent || `__EMPTY_${index + 1}`);
141:     }
142:   }
143:   return result;
144: }

146: function uniqueHeaders(headers) {
147:   const seen = new Map();
148:   return headers.map((header, index) => {
149:     const base = normalizeHeaderText(header) || `__EMPTY_${index + 1}`;
150:     const count = (seen.get(base) || 0) + 1;
151:     seen.set(base, count);
152:     return count === 1 ? base : `${base}__${count}`;
153:   });
154: }

183: function matrixToObjectRows(matrix) {
184:   const rows = Array.isArray(matrix) ? matrix : [];
185:   const headerIndex = detectHeaderRowIndex(rows);
186:   if (headerIndex < 0) return [];
187: 
188:   let dataStart = headerIndex + 1;
189:   let header = (rows[headerIndex] || []).map(normalizeHeaderText);
190:   const nextRow = rows[headerIndex + 1] || [];
191:   const parentHasGroups = header.some(Boolean) && (header.filter(Boolean).length < Math.max(header.length, nextRow.length) * 0.8 || new Set(header.filter(Boolean)).size < header.filter(Boolean).length);
192:   if (looksLikeSubHeaderRow(nextRow) && parentHasGroups) {
193:     header = composeTwoLevelHeaders(rows[headerIndex], nextRow);
194:     dataStart = headerIndex + 2;
195:   }
196:   header = uniqueHeaders(header);
197: 
198:   return rows.slice(dataStart)
199:     .filter((row) => Array.isArray(row) && row.some((value) => String(value ?? "").trim()))
200:     .map((row) => applyCanonicalHeaderAliases(Object.fromEntries(
201:       header.map((key, index) => [key, row[index] ?? ""]),
202:     )));
203: }

236: function serialToDate(value) {
237:   const serial = Number(value);
238:   if (!Number.isFinite(serial) || serial < 20000 || serial > 80000) return "";
239:   const utc = new Date(Math.round((serial - 25569) * 86400 * 1000));
240:   return `${utc.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(utc.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(utc.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
241: }
242: 
243: function localDate(value) {
244:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
245:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
246:     return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
247:   const serialDate = serialToDate(value);
248:   if (serialDate) return serialDate;
249:   const text = String(value).trim();
250:   const match = text.match(/(\d{4})[.\-/년]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\-/월]\s*(\d{1,2})/);
251:   if (match) return `${match[1]}-${match[2].padStart(2, "0")}-${match[3].padStart(2, "0")}`;
252:   const parsedText = new Date(text);
253:   if (!Number.isNaN(parsedText.getTime()) && /GMT|UTC|^[A-Za-z]{3}/.test(text))
254:     return `${parsedText.getUTCFullYear()}-${String(parsedText.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(parsedText.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
255:   return text;
256: }
257: 
258: function localTime(value) {
259:   if (value === "" || value == null) return "";
260:   if (value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))
261:     return `${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
262:   if (typeof value === "number") {
263:     const totalMinutes = Math.round((value % 1) * 24 * 60);
264:     return `${String(Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(totalMinutes % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
265:   }
266:   const text = String(value).trim();
267:   if (/^0\.\d+$/.test(text)) return localTime(Number(text));
268:   const match = text.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
269:   return match ? `${match[1].padStart(2, "0")}:${match[2]}` : text;
270: }
271: 
272: function classNumberSafe(value) { return String(value || "").replace(/[^0-9]/g, ""); }

1740: // __UEP_SELECTION_TERM_08082__
1741: function normalizeSelectionTerm(value){
1742:   const s=String(value||'').replace(/\s+/g,'').replace(/학년도/g,'').trim();
1743:   let m=s.match(/([23])(?:학년)?[-_.]?([12])(?:학기)?/);
1744:   if(!m)m=s.match(/([23])학년([12])학기/);
1745:   if(m)return `${m[1]}학년 ${m[2]}학기`;
1746:   return String(value||'').trim();
1747: }
